ParlaMint-BG_2020-03-20.u35	Madam President, ladies and gentlemen of the House! ♪ I can't believe for the first vote on a Draft Draft Law on the ratification of the Framework Agreement on the Joint Procurement of Public Procurement for Medical Counteraction Measures, No 002-02-4, submitted by the Council of Ministers on 18 March 2020. At its extraordinary meeting held on 18 March 2020, the Committee on Health examined and discussed the Draft Law on the ratification of Framework Counteraction No 002-02, submitted by the Council of Ministers on 18 March 2020. The meeting was attended by Mr. Trifon Neshkov, Director of the Directorate for Cooperation and International Cooperation of the Ministry of Health. The draft and the reasons for the draft were presented by Mr. Trifon Neshkov, who informed the MPs that the Framework Agreement on the Joint Procurement of Public Procurement for Medical Counteraction Measures (the Framework Agreement) enables the accession Member States to jointly conduct future tender procedures for specific measures for medical response............................................................................................................................................................................................................................... The Agreement was drawn up on the initiative of the European Commission as a result of discussions with experts from the Member States of the European Union in the Health Security Committee. The European Commission's initiative was linked to the difficulties of some Member States during the 2009 influenza pandemic in 2010 to provide antivirals and pandemic vaccines. The Framework Agreement shall be drawn up and signed on the basis of art. 5 of Decision No 1082/2013/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 22 October 2013 on serious cross-border threats to health and repealing Decision No 2019/98/EC. The States which accede to the Agreement shall decide to participate in a specific procedure on a case-by-case basis. The possibility of re-allocation of requested quantities is envisaged by concluding an agreement with another contracting State or to sell them to a non-member country of the Framework Agreement or a donation. The accession of the Republic of Bulgaria to the Framework Agreement will ensure better and easier access to necessary vaccines and serums, diagnostics, consumables and others under the best possible conditions in case of shortage. In the event of a crisis affecting the health of the population, the Framework Agreement allows our country to participate in new tender procedures while at the same time reserveing the right to take action on its own in order to ensure medical measures to combat it outside the joint award. It was stressed that the Agreement does not restrict the accession countries to conduct public contracts with similar and even identical items, while the States have the right to negotiate with the same counterparties producers and contractors involved in the procurement of the parties to the Framework Agreement. Mr. Neshkov clarified that the Framework Agreement provides for an opportunity to make changes to its content on a proposal from a member and to terminate participation in the Agreement without payment of compensation under the conditions laid down in the Framework Agreement. The Members of the National Assembly expressed unanimous support for the draft under consideration, stating that the ratification of the Framework Agreement is extremely timely and necessary given the emergency situation with the spread of the COVID-19 virus not only in Bulgaria but on a global scale. On the basis of the discussion and the vote carried out on the following results: for 20, without objection and abstention, the Committee on Health proposed to the National Assembly to adopt a Draft for the ratification of the Framework Agreement on the joint award of public contracts for measures for medical response, No 002-02-4, submitted by the Council of Ministers on 18 March 2020.

ParlaMint-BG_2020-03-20.u172	Mr. Chairman, ladies and gentlemen of the House! I take the floor on the fact that the proposed amendments at second reading in the Emergency Act completely contradict the text that was adopted at first reading. When we talk about the philosophy of the Law, it is not about any general arrangements, but about specific amendments. In the original version of the Bill, no role of the military from the armed forces of the Republic of Bulgaria was envisaged. The amendments, which were submitted as a second reading report, provide for art. 9, detailing the role of the Bulgarian Army in the implementation of the emergency measures. As you know, the participation of the Bulgarian Army in the civil life of the country in various forms is absolutely forbidden under the Constitution. With the exception of legislation and the Armed Forces Act, we allowed the army to participate in border guards in an extreme situation. In this regard, I would like to admire the Commission's fellow Members for having taken into account our arguments. Through this article, we largely solve the problems of individuals. However, this is not the case with legal entities, where we have a large group of companies, where the activity is virtually stopped, not only in the sectors concerned, such as hotels, restaurants, virtually every sector of the economy is currently suffering. And although there are objective reasons that make it difficult for their business, they are obliged to continue to serve their loans, the possible non-service can make them prematurely required and consequently lead to bankruptcy, to serious economic consequences for these business representatives. Moreover, we do not say imperative that there will be such a bank vacation for them, we say that in order set by the Bulgarian National Bank, these circumstances will be proven and the deadlines for their loans will be postponed. From this, banks do not lose anything, because neither reduces principal nor reduces interest rates. The only thing they will lose are some working funds that come to them monthly, but this is easy to compensate through the actions of the BNB, which can, for example, reduce the required reserves. So we don't have budget funding here, we don't have any way of damaging third parties, including commercial banks. The only thing we are creating is some sort of order to renegotiate the terms of the business loans, with which I think we will give a serious chance for him to survive in the current difficult environment. I therefore call on the National Assembly to support our proposal. By the way, his systematic place might have been around art. 6, where this matter is actually treated.

In practice, there are three hypotheses about when the employer can oblige the worker and the employee to take paid annual leave without his consent. One hypothesis is when the business of the undertaking has ceased for more than five days. The second hypothesis is when everyone in the enterprise enjoys paid annual leave. The third hypothesis is when the worker and the employee do not comply with that schedule we talked about on the use of the paid annual leave. These are valid hypotheses in the Labour Code. We need to clarify: one hypothesis that is in this article. 7, is the hypothesis of remote and home work, then the employer can oblige without the consent of the worker and the employee the use of half paid annual leave. In the next hypothesis............................................................................................... 178a We say that when there is a state of emergency, when there is a decision or order of the employer, when there is an order of the National Operational Headquarters, the employer may oblige the worker and the employee to take the paid annual leave. Let's split the entire paid annual leave. However, let's divide these hypotheses into several groups. One group is the employer obliges the worker to take his paid annual leave if, however, the undertaking, you know, there are subsequent texts. 128, if the enterprise falls on that list of the Act of the Council of Ministers, then it will be able to benefit from compensation from the Fund. However, in the other case, if it does not take advantage of this option...................................................................................................... 128, then what's he got left? The worker is left with the hypothesis to take the paid annual leave, to take, if he wishes, unpaid annual leave. The other hypothesis is that the employer organises the proceedings in such a way as to comply with the anti-epidemiology proposals of the Minister of Health. There are no other hypotheses! We need to be clear about the Civil Service Act, there is no such hypothesis of obligation. We didn't say that further. So we have said that a worker and a member of the risk groups has the right to request the use of paid annual leave and the employer cannot refuse. That's the hypothesis. We need to be clear if that's what we want to do, okay. I accept the texts in the way they are written, however, Mrs. Menda Stoyanova makes a proposal saying that when there is an emergency, the employer can oblige workers and employees to take half the leave. This proposal in the version of the Commission is edited in the second paragraph of Article 2. 7 and hence remains the feeling that in art. 178a needs to change. These are different things. Let the importers explain if we want what is recorded to remain, this is a possible hypothesis. However, if we want to implement Menda Stoyanova's proposal in the way she proposed it, not in the version of para. 2 of art. 7, these are separate things now. You understand what we're talking about, don't you? There are different hypotheses. If we clarify these things, there will be no problem with these texts, otherwise I do not think that in the way they are given there is a contradiction in the texts. They can also be implemented in this way, but this is the will of the importers, respectively of the National Assembly.

The problem is that in the current regulations, in all contracts and in everything that is happening so far, we want to change absolutely nothing, except in the conditions of the emergency situation that we are in, you know that we also have a coronavirus, and how to treat these waste specifically. Of course they're on the app, the way it is, of course the companies that are currently authorized, licensed, have the appropriate trucks, cars, transport and they will do it. The only difference in the regulation we make is that so far so-called autoclaving some of the waste is carried on an autoclave at 120 degrees, then deposited, but cannot avoid the disease and we risk those who pass through the autoclaves to infect the rest of the population. Therefore, the change in the regulation that we have specifically proposed to the Minister of Health is to proceed to burning in incinerators. What did I tell you? Two are defined and they will absorb the entire volume. What will this activity look like? All hospitals currently have contracts with their operators, licensed, registered by means of vehicles, with trained staff. The same hospitals as soon as they become Coronavirus hospitals will continue to work with the same licensed companies, with the same vehicles. They will work with the same contracts that they currently have with the incinerator in Sofia, which is national and is at our disposal, and with another one that can absorb this large volume. So, the issue of hospital waste under the regulation I accept the proposal that made the people's representative normal, we want nothing different, but simply to protect the state in a crisis, in a coronavirus situation, so that we can be sure of what remains in hospitals, that it is destroyed in the incinerators, rather than passing through autoclaves and spreading disease. On the first proposal, I have already had the opportunity to say that it concerns only and only pesticides, only those pesticides, for which we already have open warehouses and which could create public tensions, and some of them are repacked into reliable containers and started circling Europe according to our notifications. But where the trucks went, and those who are at the border, they returned them to us and said: we are a force major, we have a crisis, we stop practicing any other activity, we burn our waste from France went to Germany, Germany stood in Romania, and Romania refused us. It turns out that we have an incinerator in Bulgaria, provided with absolutely all permits, and the trucks, entering Bulgarian territory, will target them there specifically to be burned. It's not about anything outside the crisis, it's not about waste, explosives and anything else. Then write only about this kind of waste, write about hospital waste according to the regulation. I told you: the only change is that they will not autoclave at 120%, which is not reliable, but will be burned with pyrolysis in both autoclaves. The organization was set up on the ground, with refrigerated trucks, with everything else. This is a problem with which, if we don't solve it, we risk waste falling into containers and creating disease. In order not to take the floor a second time, Mr President, I apologise, if it is long, I have also made a proposal for the Minister of Health to turn to the RZI, that they turn to the mayors and district managers when there are people who are at home and in their homes, for their waste to be created an organization to be collected and eventually fall into these two incinerators, which have absolutely all the documents the Sofia is about the so-called hospital waste, and the other has all the other permits and also about hospital waste. I accept your proposal in full and instead of a dangerous one, replace with the other word, add the ordinance in accordance with you and upon the agreement that the regulation will be changed and refers to burning rather than autoclaving. Thank you.

(2) In the cases under para. 1, t. 1 Posting shall be carried out under the terms of the Civil Service Officer Act or the Labour Code in consultation with the Governor of the National Health Insurance Fund for a period of not more than two months in one calendar year. In the case of para. 1, t. 2 Posting shall be carried out under the terms of the Civil Service Officer Act in consultation with the Minister of Health for a period of not more than two months in one calendar year. Art. 19. The Minister of Health may order checks on the territory of one area of state health inspectors from another area and/or from state health inspectors at the National Centre for Radiobiology and Radiation Protection. The acts establishing administrative infringements shall be drawn up by the state health inspectors involved in the verification and the criminal decrees shall be issued by the Director of the Regional Health Inspection on whose territory the inspection is carried out.

They will be able to find themselves in a situation where they cannot serve their tax obligations. At the same time, they would not have a legal basis for their deferral. Trusting the clerical sympathy would be somewhat naive, so this text would guarantee the ability of these companies to survive and create an additional commitment of the NRA, but a commitment that, if they accepted it responsibly, could actually contribute to the survival of these businesses. In Article 4 (1) (b), point (c) is replaced by the following: 9 We propose a measure that relates to the management of speculation. It is precisely an opportunity, on a proposal from the National Operational Headquarters in the declared state of emergency, for the Minister of Economy to set marginal prices or, respectively, maximum prices of goods related to the management of the epidemic or the necessary goods respectively. We already have numerous examples, including the chairman of one of the major trade unions, which has given them a number of times increase in prices related to disinfectants and other medical supplies. Such a power, in our view, will contribute to protecting the interests of the consumer. Other colleagues have also made proposals in the direction of controlling speculation, but I believe that this is the simplest and most effective working mechanism and if it is supported by the National Assembly, it will guarantee our compatriots a truly more bearable level of prices, the means of which they depend on for their own survival. I hope for your support.

ParlaMint-BG_2020-03-20.u598	Mr. Chairman, Mr. Minister, honourable colleagues! I refer to § 6 which relates to the thus promised opportunity for workers to receive 60% of their remuneration where the undertaking has ceased to operate because of the dire situation. What the working group proposes, I respect verbatim, sounds:.. During the course of this law, but for a period of no more than three months, the National Insurance Institute shall translate 60% of the amount of insurance income for the month of January 2020 for persons insured under art. 4, para. 1 of the Social Security Code, by the insurer meeting criteria laid down by an act of the Council of Ministers. The funds shall be transferred by bank transfer to the insurance provider concerned within five working days on the basis of written information provided by the Employment Agency. (2) The funds translated according to the order of para. 1 are at the expense of the Unemployment Fund. And notice, I read the lyrics for a reason, I read para. 3, it is extremely important: If the guarantor fails to pay the full wage to the employees for whom the funds have been received, he shall reimburse them. Ladies and gentlemen, our proposal is similar, everyone sees it, but what we propose is different... the sum of these 60% directly goes to the insured person. Because we all know that the employer will have a commitment to the rest or the difference to 100%. 40%, to translate them to the insured person. And if he doesn't translate them, notice, in para. 3 You enter that the employer returns the amount. We find ourselves in the hypothesis that an employer cannot provide these 40%, there is no possibility. And we all need to understand that there are businesses in which many people work, but who have ceased to operate because it is a state of emergency and there is a minister's order. We ask them to translate the remaining difference of 40% and we tell the workers that you will not get your amount because the employer cannot take that difference. Therefore, in order to avoid this possibility and to allow people to have minimal base income, provided that the information at the National Insurance Institute has it and the employer will verify it, what prevents the amount of 60% from going directly to the insured person so that they have normal base income to survive? I am convinced that in a month's time, I hope that there will indeed be no such hypothesis, there will be many cases of returned such 60% received by employers who have been unable to ensure the difference. So reconsider your decision. And I think ours is more constructive in this critical situation. Thank you.

ParlaMint-BG_2020-03-20.u722	Dear Mr. Chairman, honourable Members! We're all tired, but I'm gonna have to ask for your attention on something very important. 28a downstairs writes: "In the Consumer Credit Act, published only when are you, art. 19th para. 4 the word "five" is replaced by "three." What's this about? It's about what's called quick loans. To recall in 2014 we placed for the first time a ceiling that interest was not five times higher than market interest. Why was it allowed five times and not twice or 16 times? Because the risk of quick loans to those who provide the funds is greater, but they still make huge profits. Now, in our decisions and the development of the situation in Bulgaria, hardly anyone doubts that more people, especially poor Bulgarian families, will be forced to resort to quick loans. Therefore, three times higher interest than the market is sufficient under these conditions for fast loans. Otherwise we will let the people go on quick loans and they will be spilled out tens of thousands of Bulgarian families. We therefore propose, instead of five times the market interest of the APRC, that the annual rate of expenditure should not be more than three times the annual APRC for the other normal loans. We will do a good deed if we save the Bulgarians from such a robbery: instead of five times three times.
